Q2.** `CaptchaWidget` · `AuthRedirect` · `PasswordRequirementsHint` migrated to Mantine. **Its finding is the sprint's most reusable:** Tailwind v4's `.text-xs` compiles to `line-height: var(--tw-leading, 1rem)`, and globals.css's `p { @apply leading-relaxed }` sets `--tw-leading` on the element — so a `<p class="text-xs">` renders **19.5px**, not 16px, with no layer conflict involved. Mantine `Text`'s own unlayered `line-height` would then override it, so `lh` had to be pinned explicitly; the `<li>` rows correctly got `1rem` instead, since no base rule targets `li`. `min-h-[60vh]` → `Center mih="60vh"`, arbitrary value removed rather than suppressed. Its colocated module is **unlayered**, so `.searchControl`'s `padding-inline: var(--space-6)` began beating Mantine `Button`'s own unlayered `.m_77c9d27d:where([data-with-left-section])` rule (`@mantine/core/styles.css:3326-3328`) that the original `px-6` had **lost** to from inside `@layer utilities`: the Search button moved 12/18px → 24/24px and **20 of 40 herosearch cells** changed md5. The executor stopped at the kickoff's own A2 checkpoint rather than patching with `!important` — the correct call. **The reviewer established a zero noise floor before attributing anything**: three post-edit runs (`T07-11`, `T08-16`, plus an owner-run `T09-04` on a separate invocation) were **0/40 md5-changed** against each other while all three were **20/40** against the pre-edit baseline, so the delta was deterministic, not harness drift; corroborated by a pixel diff isolating one 71×14 px region (543 pixels) at the button. **Two mechanism corrections came out of review, both material.** First, 709's session log attributed the win to specificity — `.searchControl` (0,1,0) beating a `:where()`-wrapped rule at (0,0,0) — which is **wrong**: `:where()` zeroes only the attribute selector it wraps, not the class chained to it, so Mantine's rule is also (0,1,0) and the module won on **source order**. The disproof was sitting in the executor's own capture: the same block's `font-weight: 600` computes to **500** both before and after, which a blanket "unlayered always wins" model cannot explain. Second, the kickoff's §3.3 claim that `sm:basis-full` "emits no rule" was an **orchestrator measurement error** (a grep that missed the `\:` escape); the drop was still correct, but on measured equivalence rather than absence. **709-R** wrapped the module in a single `@layer utilities` block and nothing else — 40 declaration lines byte-identical, only the wrapper and indentation changed. Its riskiest assumption — that `@layer` survives Next's CSS-modules pipeline with class hashing intact, unproven since no other `.module.css` in the repo used `@layer` — was made an explicit `BLOCKED` checkpoint and then **verified in the emitted CSS**, not assumed. **709-R also closed 709's evidence defect (F2):** 709 persisted `EXIT_CODE=0` beside 4 genuine FAILs; captured unpiped, the planted violation really exits **1**, so the gate does fail CI and the earlier zero was a capture artifact — handed to **710**. **`check:design-tokens` read 0, not the expected 23**, and the executor flagged it rather than quietly reporting the expected number: verified as caused by unrelated owner commit `50c40c2f8`, which tokenised exactly the four files holding all 23 violations while leaving the scanner and allowlist untouched. | 2026-07-20 | **Task 651 (Q0 doc-correction — unlayered Mantine CSS) — ✅ APPROVED (orchestrator review 2026-07-20) + COMMITTED (`docs(Task651)`).** Corrected three rows in `docs/mantine-responsive-design-system.md` (§4 CSS-import-order, §4 Tailwind-v4-layer-coexistence, §5/§6 Tailwind-boundary) that falsely claimed "`@layer mantine` is separate / Tailwind utilities can override / no conflict". Reality (confirmed Tasks 629 + 650, `layout.tsx:6`): `@mantine/core/styles.css` is imported **unlayered** (no `@layer` wrapper; `postcss-preset-mantine`'s layer option not enabled), so unlayered Mantine component CSS **beats** Tailwind `@layer utilities` — Tailwind utility classes do NOT override Mantine component styling. Added the practical rule: to make a Tailwind utility win, use the component's `unstyled` prop (Task 629) or use `Box` instead of a styled surface primitive like `Paper`/`Card` (Task 650). Docs-only (no code); mojibake 0/1847 (executor) + reviewer grep-confirmed no residual false claim + byte-integrity clean. | 2026-06-27 | **Font migration Outfit → Open Sans (Tasks 506 + 506-R + orchestrator direct fixes) ✅ APPROVED (owner rendered-proof).** Root: Outfit has no Cyrillic → uk fell back to a system font, so medium(500) labels were invisible (the actual cause of the "labels not bold" rejections; theme was never wrong). Migrated to Open Sans (`latin`+`latin-ext`+`cyrillic`+`cyrillic-ext`): `layout.tsx` loader var `--font-open-sans`, `preview-head.html` CDN `<link>`, `theme.ts` + `globals.css` font stacks. Two CSS-fallback bugs fixed: (1) **506-R** — orchestrator's 506 kickoff renamed the loader var to `--font-sans`, colliding with the `@theme` token → `--font-sans: var(--font-sans), …` self-reference → invalid → Open Sans never applied; fixed with distinct name `--font-open-sans`. (2) **orchestrator-direct** — `var(--font-open-sans), "Open Sans", …` (sibling-comma) invalidates the WHOLE declaration when the var is undefined (Storybook → Times New Roman); fixed by moving the fallback INSIDE the var: `var(--font-open-sans, "Open Sans", system-ui, …)`. Input labels 500→600 (semibold, `InputWrapper.styles.label`) — owner override since Open Sans medium ≈ regular. Owner rendered-proof @uk320: Fonts Used = Open Sans SemiBold, weight toggle now visibly changes Cyrillic. Both root causes were orchestrator kickoff CSS errors (owned). | Tasks 506, 506-R | [sessions/2026-06-27-task506R-font-variable-fix.md](sessions/2026-06-27-task506R-font-variable-fix.md) |

**Sprint 60 — Homepage: Mantine completion and Tailwind residue** (`tasks/Sprints/Sprint_60_Homepage_Mantine_Completion_And_Tailwind_Residue.md`): **752** · **753** · **754** · **755** · **756** all ✅ delivered and archived (plus 752R/756R2/756R3 reworks and **758** governance hygiene); **757** + **757R** (`AuthSheet`, Q4, critical flow) closed `APPROVED WITH NOTES` 2026-08-21. Opened 2026-08-16. Migration work, not inventory — it does **not** wait on Sprint 59, whose blockers gate a *proof* artifact, not these files. Every task names its exact files, line numbers and current `className` strings, re-read at design time. **D28 binds all six: mechanism-only, zero visual delta** — no restyle, no token change. Order 752→757 is by risk, cheapest first; **756 before 757** because the fragment `"border rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col gap-2 bg-muted/30"` is duplicated in `LocationCombobox` and `AuthSheet`, and 756 owns the decision 757 must follow. Out of scope sprint-wide: `AppImage` and `ListingFeatureIcon` (deliberate native wrappers), `PerfDevOverlay` (dev-only, `PerfDevOverlay.tsx:15`), and the container files that render no markup. ✅ COMPLETE — all tasks delivered and reviewed.
